### [111] [The Last Crossing in Excursion-Set Theory of Cosmic Reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10066)
*Hanjue Zhu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的统计量——光子计数障碍的最后穿越尺度（Rℓ），用于建模宇宙再电离过程，区分内部与外部电离区域，并揭示其密度、光源分布及莱曼α透射尖峰的物理关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于全局电离分数的模型不足以刻画星系与星系际介质（IGM）的复杂联系；需引入能反映电离光子预算与局部气体密度关系的新统计量。

Method: 发展了光子计数障碍的‘最后穿越’形式论，解析推导其尺度分布；结合Kaurov（2016）经验势垒框架与CROC数值模拟，测量模拟中首穿与末穿尺度，并对比内外电离区域的密度与星系分布特征。

Result: 发现最后穿越尺度Rℓ可有效区分外部电离（低密度、晚电离、近邻源缺失）与内部电离区域；Rℓ越大对应越低密度和越晚再电离；在固定密度与首穿尺度下，外部电离区在小尺度上星系数与电离光度偏低，中等尺度上偏高；该结构有利于莱曼α透射尖峰形成。

Conclusion: 最后穿越形式论为理解再电离时期IGM电离状态的空间异质性提供了新视角，将电离区域的内源几何结构与可观测的径向星系分布联系起来，具备可检验的观测预言。

Abstract: I introduce the last crossing of the photon-counting barrier as a statistic for analytical models of cosmic reionization. Because ionizing galaxies are biased tracers of density and their photons propagate through gas of spatially varying opacity, the galaxy-IGM connection requires statistics beyond the global ionized fraction. The excursion-set model of Furlanetto et al. (2004) identifies the ionized-bubble scale around a point with the first crossing of a photon-counting barrier. I develop the last-crossing formalism and define the last-crossing scale, $R_\ell$, as the smallest scale on which the enclosed photon budget still ionizes the enclosed gas. I derive its distribution analytically. Using the empirical-barrier framework of Kaurov (2016) applied to CROC simulations, I measure both crossings in the simulated IGM. The last-crossing formalism separates externally ionized regions, whose photon-counting condition fails below $R_\ell$, from internally ionized regions whose trajectories remain above the barrier to the resolution scale. Resolved external regions are predominantly underdense, with larger $R_\ell$ selecting lower density and later reionization. At fixed local density, first-crossing scale, and reionization redshift, these regions show deficits in ionizing-luminosity and galaxy-number density at small radius relative to matched unresolved regions, and excesses in both at intermediate radius. Their combination of low gas density and a deficit of nearby sources suggests that Ly$α$ transmission spikes may preferentially arise in these regions: low density reduces the opacity, while sources on larger scales maintain the ionized state. The last-crossing picture connects the internal source geometry of ionized regions to a testable prediction for radial galaxy distributions around transmission-selected IGM locations.

</details>


### [112] [Lyman Break Galaxy selection and redshift measurement with supervised contrastive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10080)
*J. Choppin de Janvry,C. Yèche,Arjun Dey,C. Magneville,C. Payerne,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,E. Armengaud,S. Bailey,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,D. Brooks,A. Carnero Rosell,E. Chaussidon,T. Claybaugh,A. Cuceu,K. S. Dawson,A. de la Macorra,A. de Mattia,P. Doel,A. Font-Ribera,J. E. Forero-Romero,E. Gaztañaga,Satya Gontcho A Gontcho,G. Gutierrez,H. K. Herrera-Alcantar,K. Honscheid,M. Ishak,S. Juneau,T. Karim,D. Kirkby,A. Kremin,A. Lambert,M. Landriau,L. Le Guillou,M. Manera,A. Meisner,R. Miquel,S. Nadathur,G. Niz,E. Paillas,N. Palanque-Delabrouille,W. J. Percival,F. Prada,I. Pérez-Ràfols,C. Ravoux,G. Rossi,R. Ruggeri,E. Sanchez,C. Saulder,D. Schlegel,M. Schubnell,H. Seo,J. Silber,M. Siudek,G. Tarlé,B. A. Weaver,H. Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督加权对比学习的方法，用于高红移星系光谱的红移估计与样本净化（剔除类星体和低红移发射线星系），在小规模人工标注数据上表现优于原有QuasarNET模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI Run 2将探测z~2–4.5的微弱莱曼断裂星系（LBG），但其光谱红移测量和样本污染（混入类星体、低红移发射线星系）问题突出，尤其在目标选择后仍需有效净化。

Method: 提出监督加权对比学习框架，将红移回归与分类（类星体/星系判别）联合建模；通过引入连续关系权重改进对比损失，使网络同时学习红移表征与类别区分。

Result: 在相同数据集上，该模型在异常样本（如污染源）分类能力上显著强于改进版QuasarNET，红移估计精度相当；且在小规模目视检验样本上展现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 监督加权对比学习是一种适用于小样本、多任务天文光谱分析的有效方法，为高红移星系巡天的数据处理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Some of the next steps for high-precision cosmology lie within the high-redshift, high-density universe. Spectroscopic survey experiments such as the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI)'s second phase DESI Run 2 will shift towards probing Lyman Break Galaxy (LBG) populations from z$\sim$2 to z$\sim$4.5. For this faint sample, spectroscopic redshift measurement and sample decontamination remains a challenge, even after target selection. We propose an approach based on supervised weighted contrastive learning, in order to both learn a redshift representation for spectra and decontaminate the sample from quasars and low redshift emission line galaxies. This strategy generalizes the contrastive learning loss approach with continuous relationship weights, such that the network simultaneously learns redshift and classification tasks. The model shows stronger outlier classification and comparable redshift identification performances when compared to the previous network used for DESI (a modified version of QuasarNET) on the same dataset. In particular, contrastive learning is well suited to the small, visually-inspected sample used for training and testing, especially given the multi-task nature of this work.

</details>


### [113] [Reconstructing Dark Matter Mass and Discriminating Standard and Non-Standard WIMP-Nucleus Interactions with Paleo-Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10105)
*Dionysios P. Theodosopoulos,Katherine Freese,Chris Kelso,Patrick Stengel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次详细研究了古探测器在暗物质信号存在下重建暗物质参数及区分不同暗物质-原子核相互作用类型的能力，表明其在低质量（<10 GeV）和高质量（达1 TeV）WIMP探测方面优于传统直接探测实验，并能在无核反冲方向测量条件下区分标准与非标准NREFT相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直接探测实验在低质量暗物质探测及相互作用类型区分上存在局限，而古探测器利用地质时间尺度积累的晶格损伤，有望提供互补且更强的参数重建与模型区分能力。

Method: 基于代表性核反冲径迹读出方案，结合非相对论有效场论（NREFT）框架，对弹性与非弹性暗物质-原子核散射信号进行参数重建与模型区分能力的统计分析。

Result: 古探测器可重建WIMP质量范围约<10 GeV至1 TeV，较传统实验扩展约2倍；在≥10 GeV质量区间，无需核反冲方向测量即可排除多数非标准NREFT相互作用，实现对标准自旋无关/自旋相关相互作用的显著区分。

Conclusion: 古探测器不仅拓展了暗物质质量探测范围，还在无方向信息前提下展现出优异的相互作用类型分辨能力，为暗物质物理提供独特且不可替代的探针。

Abstract: Paleo-detectors record and retain crystal damage in ancient minerals from nuclear recoils induced by dark matter scattering over geological timescales. Previous studies have shown that paleo-detectors can provide sensitivity to a variety of dark matter (DM) scenarios which is complementary to conventional direct-detection experiments. In this paper, we complete the first detailed study of how well paleo-detectors can reconstruct DM parameters or distinguish between different types of DM interactions with nuclei in the presence of a DM signal, considering both elastic and inelastic DM-nucleus scattering. For representative nuclear recoil track read-out scenarios, we demonstrate that weakly interacting massive particle (WIMP) DM masses can be reconstructed for a variety of Non-Relativistic Effective Field Theory (NREFT) interactions between WIMPs and nuclei. In particular, paleo-detectors are projected to be capable of reconstructing WIMP masses $\lesssim$ 10 GeV, a regime that is challenging for conventional direct-detection experiments; further, we find that paleo-detectors could reconstruct WIMP masses up to 1 TeV for hypothetical signals within their accessible parameter space, extending the mass range over which reconstruction is possible by up to a factor of $\sim 2$ compared with analogous studies of conventional direct-detection experiments. In addition, we demonstrate that paleo-detectors could discriminate between canonical spin-independent or spin-dependent NREFT interactions and non-canonical interactions which can depend on the relative velocity or momentum transferred between the WIMP and nucleus. Specifically, at WIMP masses $\gtrsim$ 10 GeV, we project that canonical NREFT interactions can be excluded by paleo-detectors in the cases of nearly all non-canonical interactions without measurement of nuclear recoil direction, which conventional experiments typically require.

</details>


### [114] [Evaporation of Primordial Black Holes with Multimodal Mass and Extended Spin Distributions: Cosmological Imprints on the Effective Number of Relativistic Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10127)
*T. Toghrai,A. Daassou,Y. Ouchhaine,H. Laassiri,R. Benbrik*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FRISHBEE代码，用于模拟多种形成机制下的原初黑洞（PBH）质量函数，并研究其对有效中微子种类数（ΔN_eff）的影响，发现扩展质量函数显著增强该信号，且自旋与暗辐射类型会改变信号强弱关系，从而为区分PBH单/多通道形成机制提供观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一质量近似会抹去不同PBH形成机制所留下的特征质量谱信息，需构建更真实的多模态质量函数模型以连接通胀模型与CMB可观测量。

Method: 基于FRISBHEE代码开发FRISHBEE，实现四种质量函数（对数正态、幂律、临界坍缩、度规预热）及两种自旋分布（高斯、Fishbach），权重由原初坍缩概率导出；求解Friedmann-Boltzmann方程，计算不同质量、自旋与加权方案下的ΔN_eff。

Result: 扩展质量函数使ΔN_eff比单色基准增强1.03–1.84倍；自旋接近极值（a_⋆=0.99）和自旋-2暗辐射下，单色近似反而高估ΔN_eff；在CMB-S4/Simons Observatory灵敏度下，仅对数正态、幂律和多模态分布可达~1.6–1.7σ，而自旋-2暗辐射下信号衰减超一个数量级。

Conclusion: PBH形成通道决定霍金辐射信号的幅度与可观测性，ΔN_eff可作为区分单通道与多通道PBH形成机制的关键观测判据。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) form through several distinct mechanisms, each imprinting a characteristic mass function that a single-mass treatment risks erasing. Building on the FRISBHEE code, we introduce FRISHBEE, implementing four mass functions--log-normal, power-law, critical collapse, metric preheating--in a multimodal framework, with spin modeled via Gaussian and Fishbach profiles. Crucially, each channel's weight is derived from the primordial collapse probability rather than fitted, tying the mass function to the power-spectrum amplitude--a link between inflationary model-building and CMB observables. Solving the Friedmann-Boltzmann equations for $M_{\rm PBH}^{\rm in}=10^{7}$~g in the pre-BBN window, we compute $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ for five distributions, three spin configurations, and three weightings. Extended mass functions enhance $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ over the monochromatic benchmark by factors of $\sim\!1.03$ (critical collapse) to $\sim\!1.84$ (log-normal), preserving the hierarchy $LN > MM > PL > MP > CC$ across the mass window $\{10^{5},10^{7},10^{8}\}$~g. Near-extremal spin ($a_{\star}=0.99$) and spin-2 dark radiation invert this trend: superradiant spin loss averages away faster in broad distributions than in a monochromatic population, letting the monochromatic approximation overestimate $ΔN_{\rm eff}$. Against CMB-S4 and Simons Observatory sensitivities ($σ\simeq 0.06$ and $0.05$), the monochromatic benchmark is undetectable for scalar dark radiation (sDR $=0$), while log-normal, power-law, and multimodal distributions reach $\sim\!1.6$--$1.7σ$; for spin-2 dark radiation the signal drops over an order of magnitude, with none detectable. The formation channel thus governs the magnitude and observational accessibility of the Hawking signal, establishing $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ as a discriminant between single- and multi-channel PBH formation scenarios.

</details>


### [115] [CORN -- Chronometers of Relic Nature I: The first estimate of the expansion rate of the Universe using compact relic galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10163)
*Krzysztof Lisiecki,Nicola Principi Cavaterra,Agnieszka Pollo,Chiara Spiniello,Laura Hunt,Charlie Rosen,Marek Biesiada,Darko Donevski,Patryk Matera,Giuliano Lorenzon,Katarzyna Małek John Mills*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用宇宙计时器（CC）方法，通过分析古老、致密的超紧凑大质量星系（UCMGs）的恒星年龄演化，独立测量了红移z=0.15处的哈勃参数H(z)，得到H(z=0.15) = 85±53 km/s/Mpc，旨在缓解哈勃常数H0在早期与晚期宇宙探针间的张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前宇宙学中早期与晚期探针对哈勃常数H0测量结果之间显著不一致（即哈勃张力）的问题，亟需独立、模型无关的测量方法以检验系统误差。

Method: 采用宇宙计时器（CC）方法，聚焦于作为‘化石遗迹’的超紧凑大质量星系（UCMGs），其拥有最古老的恒星种群，从而最大程度抑制恒星形成历史和并合历史带来的年龄测定不确定性。

Result: 获得红移z=0.15处独立的哈勃参数测量值：H(z=0.15) = 85±53 km/s/Mpc。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了基于古老UCMGs的CC方法在缓解哈勃张力中的潜力，虽当前误差较大，但为未来高精度观测提供了新路径。

Abstract: Measuring the expansion rate of the Universe is a central challenge in cosmology, particularly due to the persistent statistically significant discrepancy between the measurements of the Hubble constant, H0, obtained using early- and late-time probes, commonly known as the Hubble tension. Independent and robust methods are therefore essential to validate existing measurements and assess potential systematic effects.
  We employ the cosmic chronometer (CC) approach, which uses the differential age evolution of quiescent galaxies to measure the Hubble parameter H(z) without assuming an underlying cosmological model. In contrast to previous CC studies, we restrict our analysis to relics -- ultra compact massive galaxies (UCMGs) hosting the oldest stellar populations -- thereby minimising uncertainties related to star formation history and merger history.
  We employ the cosmic chronometer (CC) approach, which uses the differential age evolution of quiescent galaxies to measure the Hubble parameter H(z) without assuming an underlying cosmological model. In contrast to previous CC studies, we restrict our analysis to relics -- ultra compact massive galaxies (UCMGs) hosting the oldest stellar populations -- thereby minimising uncertainties related to star formation history and merger history.
  We obtain an independent measurement of H(z=0.15) = 85\pm53 km/s/Mpc.

</details>


### [116] [Evidence for Enhancement in the Rate of Fast Radio Bursts Toward Galaxy Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10358)
*Mawson W. Sammons,Airene Ahuja,Matt Dobbs,Zarif Kader,Evan Davies-Velie,Mohit Bhardwaj,Charanjot Brar,Amanda M. Cook,Alice P. Curtin,Bryan M. Gaensler,Affan Khadir,Victoria M. Kaspi,Afrokk Khan,Adam Lanman,Calvin Leung,Lluis Mas-Ribas,Kiyoshi W. Masui,Kyle McGregor,Daniele Michilli,Ayush Pandhi,Aaron B. Pearlman,Ziggy Pleunis,Sachin Pradeep E. T.,Aniket Prasad,J. Xavier Prochaska,Paul Scholz,Kaitlyn Shin,Kendrick Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究发现CHIME/FRB第二版基带目录中约1.4%的快速射电暴（FRBs）与已知星系团存在空间关联，统计显著性达3σ，表明星系团方向FRB出现率显著增强；该增强可能源于星系团成员星系产生额外FRB及引力透镜放大背景源两种机制，并可用于约束FRB前身星模型和高红移演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团可通过引力透镜效应和抑制成员星系恒星形成，改变视线方向上的可观测源分布；因此，与星系团对齐的FRB可提供独特视角，探索FRB种群中其他视线无法触及的参数空间。

Method: 利用DECaLS最新数据识别星系团，匹配CHIME/FRB第二版基带目录中的FRB，筛选出26个很可能位于或后方于星系团的FRB样本（含1个重复暴、2个穿过后发座星系团的FRB）；通过对比多种模拟FRB种群，评估观测到的关联数的统计显著性。

Result: 观测到的FRB–星系团关联数比随机预期高出3σ，占CHIME/FRB第二版目录总FRB数的1.4±0.4%；FRB 20211113A与强引力透镜Abell 2218对齐，被识别为潜在透镜候选体。

Conclusion: FRB在星系团方向的超额出现支持成员星系贡献额外FRB及引力透镜放大两种物理机制；该现象对FRB前身星通道和高红移演化敏感，有望成为未来限制这些关键特性的新探针。

Abstract: Massive galaxy clusters can introduce gravitational lensing and populations of suppressed star formation member galaxies into the line of sight, potentially changing the distribution of observable sources toward them from that of the average sky. As a result, Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) aligned with galaxy clusters can provide a unique window into parameter spaces of the FRB population that other lines of sight do not afford. In this study, we demonstrate that the second CHIME/FRB baseband catalog contains FRBs emitted from within or behind clusters identified from the latest DECaLS data release; we isolate a sample of 26 FRBs where this is likely, including one repeating FRB and two FRBs that intersect the Coma cluster. Comparing our results against a range of simulated FRB populations, we conclude that the number of these associations represents a $3σ$ rate enhancement toward galaxy clusters, constituting $1.4\pm0.4\%$ of the FRBs detected in the second CHIME/FRB baseband catalog. We suggest that this enhancement is caused by an equal proportion of member galaxies hosting additional FRBs, and gravitational lensing magnifying background sources. We demonstrate that such contributions are sensitive to alternative progenitor channels and high redshift evolution in the FRB population, providing a future avenue for constraining these features. Considering the redshifts and masses of the associated clusters, we identify FRB 20211113A, which is aligned with the strong gravitational lens Abell 2218 as a potential lensed candidate for further consideration.

</details>


### [117] [Evolution of Neutron Star Environment in the Galactic Halo : Implications for Dark Matter Accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10781)
*Payaswinee Arvikar,Aseem Paranjape,Saee Dhawalikar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了银河系晕中动态暗物质环境密度对中子星暗物质吸积的影响，发现亚结构动力学仅能将中子星中的暗物质质量提高约2倍，不足以解释状态方程估计与吸积估计之间数量级的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释中子星中暗物质质量的状态方程估计（约10^{-2} M_{\odot}）与吸积估计（约10^{-14} M_{\odot}）之间巨大差异的物理机制。

Method: 利用高分辨率N体模拟和Voronoi镶嵌法，统计计算银河系类似物中假想中子星位置附近的局部暗物质密度，评估亚结构动力学对暗物质吸积的增强效应。

Result: 亚结构动力学可使中子星中暗物质质量相较球对称吸积基准提高约2倍，但远不足以弥合数量级差距。

Conclusion: 环境效应（如暗物质晕亚结构）无法解释状态方程与吸积模型在中子星暗物质含量上的巨大分歧，需探索其他物理机制。

Abstract: Neutron stars (NS) are one of the indirect detection probes for dark matter (DM). The presence of DM is known to affect the observable properties of NS. Theoretical calculations for various equations of state of a DM admixed NS lead to estimates of the DM mass in a NS of the order of $10^{-2} M_{\odot}$. On the other hand, simplistic estimates of the amount of DM that is accreted on to the NS in the Solar neighborhood, over its age, suggest that this number is of the order $10^{-14} M_{\odot}$. Various studies have addressed this non-agreement theoretically by explaining the mechanisms leading to higher fraction than expected from smooth spherically symmetric accretion. In this work, we attempt to assess the role of the dynamic DM environmental density in the Galactic halo to explain possible enhancement in DM accretion. We consider a high resolution N-body simulation and method of Voronoi tessellation to calculate local DM density around putative NS locations in a statistically representative sample of Milky Way analogues. We infer that the dynamics of substructure may enhance the DM mass in NS by about a factor 2 as compared to the baseline, spherically symmetric expectation. Environmental effects therefore cannot explain the orders of magnitude discrepancy between equation of state and accretion based estimates of DM admixed in NS.

</details>


### [118] [Fitting trends in quasar emission and absorption line redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10945)
*Netra K Subramanian,Prasad Subramanian,Nimisha G Kantharia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了类星体中发射线与吸收线红移之间的系统性关系，提出了 MgII 和 CIV 吸收线最低红移随发射线红移变化的经验模型，并验证了其高拟合优度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的吸收线红移 z_abs 相对于发射线红shift z_em 的系统性趋势，检验其是否源于类星体自身而非星系际介质。

Method: 基于大样本类星体的 MgII 和 CIV 吸收线红移数据，拟合最低吸收红移随发射红移变化的经验线性关系，并评估拟合优度（R²）。

Result: 得到 MgII 最低红移模型：z_MgIImodel = (0.418±0.008)z_em − (0.482±0.02)，R²=0.99；CIV 模型：z_CIVmodel = 0.845(±0.0002)z_em − 0.153(±0.0006)。

Conclusion: MgII 和 CIV 吸收线最低红移与发射红移存在强线性相关，支持这些吸收特征可能起源于类星体本身或其近邻环境，而非随机分布的星系际介质。

Abstract: The spectrum of a quasar consists of a few emission lines whose wavelengths are shifted by similar redshifts and numerous absorption lines whose wavelengths are shifted by different redshifts. Hence each quasar is characterised by an emission line redshift and the absorption lines redshifts are all less than the emission line redshift. The distribution of observed absorption line redshifts ($z_{abs}$) with respect to emission line redshift ($z_{em}$) for a large sample of quasars shows a systematic trend as pointed out by \citet{2016arXiv160901593K}. They noticed that increase in $z_{em}$ is accompanied by a monotonic increase in the lowest detected value of $z_{abs}$ and inferred that the emission and absorption lines were all formed in the quasar.
  This study focuses on modeling the systematic trend in the observed $z_{em} \rightarrow z_{abs}$ distribution. We considered the redshift data of absorption lines of singly ionized magnesium (denoted by MgII) and triply ionized carbon (denoted by CIV) for a large sample of quasars. We find that the envelope of data points defining the lowest value of the MgII absorption line redshift (which we denote by $z_{MgIImodel}$) for a given $z_{em}$ satisfies $z_{MgIImodel} = (0.418 \pm 0.008) z_{em} - (0.482 \pm 0.02)$ with an $R^2$ value of 0.99. The model can be used to predict the lowest expected MgII absorption line redshift for any $z_{em}$. We find a similar model for the lowest expected redshift of triply ionized carbon lines for any $z_{em}$ which is $z_{CIVmodel} = 0.845 (\pm 0.0002) z_{em} - 0.153 (\pm 0.0006)$.

</details>


### [119] [Fine-tuning in mixed Dark Matter models with Primordial Black Hole relics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10977)
*Amirah Aljazaeri,Christian T. Byrnes*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了由超轻原初黑洞（PBHs）主导的三元暗物质模型的参数精细调节问题，发现PBH遗迹丰度计算本身是主要调谐来源，而非粒子暗物质候选者或PBH蒸发细节；无论辐射主导还是PBH主导宇宙学，以及不同PBH形成机制（如相变、畴壁坍缩），均存在指数级敏感依赖，使得PBH遗迹丰度接近1的自然实现极为困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三元暗物质场景（含超轻PBH及其遗迹、PBH蒸发产生的DM粒子、独立产生的DM组分）中参数精细调节的程度，以评估其自然性。

Method: 系统分析PBH形成（通过进入视界后的密度扰动坍缩）、蒸发及遗迹产生过程，并结合热WIMP冻结、冻结产生及QCD轴子误直机制，统一评估再现观测暗物质丰度Ω_DM所需的参数敏感性；同时考察早期PBH主导时期的影响及替代PBH形成机制（如一级相变、畴壁坍缩）。

Result: PBH遗迹丰度计算的结构主导精细调节；辐射主导宇宙中，暗物质丰度对原初曲率功率谱呈指数敏感；早期PBH主导虽稀释原有丰度，但通胀初始大振幅扰动仍需精细调节；其他PBH形成机制则引入替代性的指数敏感性。

Conclusion: 自然地实现O(1)量级的PBH遗迹丰度非常困难。

Abstract: We investigate the fine-tuning of a tripartite dark matter (DM) scenario involving ultra-light primordial black holes (PBHs), whose evaporation before Big Bang Nucleosynthesis leaves Planck-mass relics and produces DM particles together with an independently produced DM component. We uniformly evaluate the parameter sensitivity required to reproduce the observed DM abundance, $Ω_{\rm DM}$. Considering thermal WIMP freeze-out, freeze-in, and QCD axion misalignment, and assuming PBHs form via collapse of perturbations following horizon entry, we find that the fine-tuning is normally dominated by the structure of the PBH relic abundance calculation rather than by the particle DM candidate or the details of PBH evaporation. In radiation-dominated cosmologies, the DM abundance is highly sensitive to the primordial curvature power spectrum because the PBH formation fraction depends exponentially on density fluctuations. Although an early PBH-dominated era dilutes pre-existing abundances and reduces the apparent tuning of the PBH abundance, inflationary fine-tuning remains required to produce the required initial large amplitude perturbations. We further examine alternative PBH formation channels, including supercooled first-order phase transitions and collapsing domain walls, and find that they replace the inflationary fine-tuning with alternative exponential sensitivities. We conclude that a natural realization of an order unity PBH relic abundance is hard to motivate.

</details>
